### [1] [JetParticle-JEPA: An Efficient Self-Supervised Representation Learning method for Jet Tagging in High-Energy Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14813)
*Guillaume Letellier,Antonin Vacheret,Frédéric Jurie*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: JP-JEPA 是一种基于粒子变换器的自监督联合嵌入预测架构，直接从连续粒子云学习物理意义明确的喷注表征，无需分词或重建原始输入，在 JetClass 和 Top/Quark-Gluon 标准测试中达到或超越监督方法性能，并展现出对探测器建模误差和缺失信息的强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低喷注标记任务对大规模模拟数据的依赖，缓解计算开销高与探测器建模失配导致的鲁棒性不足问题。

Method: 采用自监督 Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture（JEPA），以 Particle Transformer 为骨干网络，对粒子云进行掩码建模，预测被掩码粒子的潜在表征，保持细粒度运动学关联。

Result: 在 JetClass 上全数据集性能媲美 SOTA 监督模型，在低标签量下显著优于监督基线；在 Top Quark 和 Quark-Gluon Tagging 上与监督方法持平；表征对缺失探测器信息具强鲁棒性，不确定性行为更优。

Conclusion: JP-JEPA 为 LHC 喷注物理提供了兼具鲁棒性与数据效率的基础模型新范式。

Abstract: Jet tagging at the Large Hadron Collider increasingly relies on deep learning models trained on massive simulated datasets, leading to high computational costs and limited robustness to detector mismodeling. We introduce JetParticle-JEPA (JP-JEPA), a self-supervised Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture that learns physically meaningful jet representations directly from continuous particle clouds without tokenization or reconstruction of raw inputs. Built on a Particle Transformer backbone, JP-JEPA predicts latent representations of masked particles while preserving fine-grained kinematic correlations. On the JetClass benchmark, JP-JEPA achieves performance comparable to fully supervised state-of-the-art methods on the full dataset, surpasses supervised baselines in low-label regimes, and significantly outperforms existing SSL approaches. On Top Quark and Quark-Gluon Tagging benchmarks, it remains on par with supervised methods. The learned representations also exhibit strong robustness to missing detector information and improved uncertainty behavior, highlighting JP-JEPA as a promising foundation-model framework for robust and data-efficient jet physics at the LHC.

</details>


### [2] [Adiabatic response in the Migdal Effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14845)
*Stefan Nellen Mondragón,Josef Pradler,Mukul Sholapurkar*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次基于第一性原理计算了孤立原子中的Migdal效应，揭示了其在绝热交叉区域的电离抑制行为，并证明该行为完全由散射振幅决定，无需人为假设；同时划定了相关参数空间，指出当前暗物质直接探测实验处于电离未被抑制的区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清Migdal效应在超出冲量近似下的有效性问题，因其广泛应用于直接暗物质搜寻但理论基础尚不完善。

Method: 采用第一性原理方法对孤立原子进行计算，严格处理核反冲引发的电子激发过程，分析散射振幅与电离概率的关系，并确定绝热-非绝热 crossover 的条件。

Result: 发现存在一个由核反冲能量和电子束缚能共同决定的绝热 crossover 区域，在此区域内Migdal电离被显著抑制；该抑制行为完全由散射振幅解析编码；当前主流暗物质实验参数位于非抑制区域。

Conclusion: Migdal效应在典型暗物质探测能量尺度下仍有效，其理论基础可通过散射振幅严格确立，无需引入经验假设；绝热抑制仅在极端低能或强束缚条件下显现。

Abstract: The Migdal effect-the prompt ionization induced by a sudden nuclear recoil-is widely used in direct dark matter searches, yet its validity beyond the impulse approximation has remained unresolved. We present the first first-principles calculation for isolated atoms, establishing the adiabatic crossover where ionization is suppressed. We show that this behavior is fully encoded in the scattering amplitude, without ad hoc assumptions, and map the relevant parameter space, finding that dark matter searches lie in the unsuppressed regime.

</details>


### [3] [Event Generation with Parallel Langevin Sampling and Learned Stein Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14854)
*Rob Verheyen*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于并行欠阻尼Langevin链的高效事件生成方法，利用学习型Stein差异作为收敛诊断，显著减少高重数末态下精确矩阵元计算次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高重数末态下的精确矩阵元计算昂贵且传统拒绝采样效率低下，亟需更高效的无权重事件生成算法。

Method: 采用多条并行欠阻尼Langevin动力学链，每链仅保留终端状态以获得无权重事件；引入学习型Stein discrepancy作为数据驱动的弛豫时间估计器；结合神经网络代理初始化加速收敛。

Result: 在树图阶uū→Z+ng过程上验证该方法，发现弛豫所需精确目标Langevin步数仅随末态粒子数缓慢增长；神经网络代理初始化大幅降低精确矩阵元及梯度求值次数。

Conclusion: 该方法为高精度对撞机唯象学提供了可扩展、低计算开销的无权重事件生成新范式，尤其适用于高重数QCD末态。

Abstract: Efficient event generation is a major computational challenge for precision collider phenomenology, especially for high-multiplicity final states where matrix-element evaluations are expensive and rejection-sampling efficiencies are low. We study an alternative approach based on many parallel underdamped Langevin chains, retaining one terminal state from each chain to obtain unweighted events while avoiding within-chain autocorrelation. A learned Stein discrepancy is used as a convergence diagnostic, providing a data-driven estimate of the relaxation time. We apply the method to tree-level $u\bar u\to Z+n g$ event generation and find that relaxation requires only a modest number of exact-target Langevin steps, with mild growth over the multiplicities studied. Finally, we show that simple neural-network surrogate initialization can substantially reduce the required number of exact matrix-element and gradient evaluations.

</details>


### [4] [Towards a consistent perturbation theory at finite temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14863)
*Passant Ali,Peter Lowdon,Owe Philipsen*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于热粒子（thermoparticles）概念的有限温度量子场论微扰理论新框架，修正了传统Gell-Mann-Low关系在热场论中的不一致推广，消除了实时间形式论中病态的壳层贡献和无质量理论中的红外发散问题，并通过与格点模拟的两圈虚时间计算对比，验证了其对φ⁴理论两点关联函数的高精度预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准有限温度量子场论微扰方法存在实时间形式论中病态壳层贡献及无质量理论红外发散等问题，根源在于Gell-Mann-Low关系在热场论中不恰当的推广——错误地使用自由散射态，而相互作用热系统中不存在此类态。

Method: 基于非微扰谱学洞察，将有限温度散射态建模为阻尼但稳定的类粒子激发（即thermoparticles），据此重构Gell-Mann-Low关系；由此导出的微扰展开具有与标准方法相同拓扑结构，但传播子替换为依赖动力学的thermoparticle传播子。

Result: 该thermoparticle微扰理论彻底消除了标准方法中的壳层奇异性与红外发散；在质量φ⁴理论中，其两圈虚时间计算结果与格点数值模拟的两点关联函数高度吻合，展现出与真空微扰理论相当的定量精度。

Conclusion: Gell-Mann-Low关系在热场论中必须以非微扰谱结构（thermoparticles）为基础进行重构；thermoparticle微扰理论不仅解决了长期存在的基础性问题，而且具备实际可计算性与高精度预测能力，为有限温度强耦合动力学提供了严格微扰框架。

Abstract: The standard approach to perturbation theory for finite-temperature quantum field theories has several issues, including the appearance of ill-defined on-shell contributions in the real-time formulation, and infrared diverges in massless theories. Earlier studies indicate that these issues all stem from the inconsistent thermal generalisation of the Gell-Mann-Low relation, which forms the foundation of perturbation theory in vacuum. This inconsistency arises from the use of free scattering states in the relation, which are known not to exist in interacting thermal theories. In this work, we propose a generalisation of the Gell-Mann-Low relation for scalar theories based on non-perturbative spectral insights, namely that finite-temperature scattering states can be described by damped but stable particle-like excitations, so-called thermoparticles. The perturbative expansion of this generalised relation gives rise to contributions with exactly the same topology as the standard finite-temperature approach, except that now the propagators appearing in this expansion are not those of a free field but of thermoparticles, which depend on the dynamics of the theory. We demonstrate that thermoparticle perturbation theory resolves the known problems of the standard approach. Furthermore, by comparing imaginary-time calculations at two-loop level with numerical lattice simulations of two-point correlation functions in massive $φ^{4}$ theory, we explicitly show that this framework gives rise to precise predictions, as in the vacuum case, in stark contrast to the standard approach.

</details>


### [5] [Why fluctuations of conserved charges in the confining regime above $T_{ch}$ behave as if the quarks were free?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15798)
*L. Ya. Glozman*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在手征交叉温度T_ch以上、但低于去禁闭温度T_d的QCD介质中，守恒荷涨落与介子关联函数表现出的不同行为：前者近似自由夸克气体，后者则体现手征自旋和SU(4)对称性，揭示色单态束缚态主导的动力学；作者指出守恒荷密度的空间传播子近似自由夸克环，而时间传播（即介子传播子）则显著偏离，从而阐明‘守恒荷不感知禁闭’这一现象，并提出‘弦状流体’微观图像——由重叠强相互作用色单态团簇构成，泡利原理驱动的夸克交换导致其准自由化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释为何在手征 crossover 温度附近，守恒荷涨落呈现自由夸克行为（被误读为去禁闭证据），而介子关联函数却显示非微扰色单态结构，二者表观矛盾需统一微观理解。

Method: 对比分析全QCD中守恒荷密度的空间传播子与介子（如矢量/赝标量）关联函数的行为，结合零温夸克-强子对偶性类比，构建基于色单态团簇与夸克交换的‘弦状流体’有效图像。

Result: 守恒荷密度的空间传播子在T > 220 MeV时与自由夸克环高度一致，而介子传播子则显著偏离；该差异源于守恒荷不参与时间演化传播（无动力学束缚），而介子作为色单态激发具有非微扰时空传播特性；由此确立‘守恒荷盲于禁闭’的基本原理。

Conclusion: 在T_ch < T < T_d区间，QCD介质并非去禁闭夸克胶子等离子体，而是由强耦合色单态团簇构成的弦状流体；守恒荷涨落的‘自由性’源于夸克在团簇间泡利交换导致的准自由化，而非真实去禁闭；该图像统一协调涨落与谱函数观测，深化对强耦合QCD相结构的理解。

Abstract: Some cumulants of the fluctuations of conserved charges soon above the chiral crossover behave as if the quarks were free. This was taken by many as evidence of deconfinement. At the same temperatures the mesonic correlators reveal the chiral spin and SU(4) symmetries, indicating that the propagating degrees of freedom are massless quarks connected into color singlets by the chromoelectric confining string. These correlators are qualitatively different from the free quark gas. Here we clarify the reason for the difference. The conserved quark number densities do not propagate in time but do propagate in spatial directions. The mesonic propagators calculated in full QCD differ radically from the free quark loop (quark gas) above T_ch. In contrast, the quark number density spatial propagator in full QCD at T > 220 MeV is very close to the free quark loop.
  In other words, the conserved charges do not see confinement, in contrast to the mesonic correlators. This is consistent with the well understood quark-hadron duality at T=0 in e^+e^- -> hadrons, where at invariant masses above 2 GeV the cross-section in the confining regime is represented by the free quark loop plus small perturbative corrections. All these features above T_ch but below the deconfinement temperature T_d can be combined within the following microscopic picture of the stringy fluid matter. It is a medium of the overlapping strongly interacting color singlet clusters. The quark interchanges between the clusters, required by Paili principle, make the quarks quasifree, which is reflected in fluctuations of conserved charges.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为未来高能电子-离子对撞实验（如EIC、LHeC、FCC-eh）提供高精度、多末态一致的强子级DIS理论预言，以支持精确QCD检验与核结构研究。

Result: 给出了适用于EIC、LHeC和FCC-eh能量标下的NLO精度强子级DIS微分截面预言，覆盖多种末态多重数，具备直接用于实验分析的事件样本。

Abstract: In this contribution we discuss state-of-the-art hadron-level predictions for the deep-inelastic scattering process at next-to-leading-order precision for several multiplicities, consistently merged in one sample. We focus on the physics at (potential) future colliders, the Electron-Ion Collider planned at BNL as well as the higher energy experiments discussed as future options at CERN, a LHeC and a DIS phase of the Future Circular Collider dubbed FCC-eh.

</details>


### [7] [Chiral-odd structure of the $N \to Δ$ transition: tensor form factors from QCD light-cone sum rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16433)
*Ulaş Özdem*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次利用QCD光锥求和规则直接计算了N→Δ跃迁的张量跃迁形状因子（TFFs），基于洛伦兹协变性、厄米性、宇称、时间反演及Rarita-Schwinger约束导出四个独立形式因子；通过核子分布振幅在不同扭度下的展开，在1–10 GeV²类空动量转移区间计算TFFs，并外推至静态极限；发现u/d夸克贡献呈现三种不同味结构：F₁,F₂中d夸克主导，F₃具近似反对称味结构（Fᵘ≈−Fᵈ），F₄中u/d贡献大小相当但符号相反；这些TFFs提供手征奇（chiral-odd）信息，可为未来横自旋相关跃迁可观测量提供模型无关输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 获取N→Δ跃迁中手征奇张量流的非微扰信息，弥补电磁与引力跃迁所缺失的chiral-odd通道，并为横自旋观测量提供基础输入，同时检验不同QCD方法（如格点QCD）间的一致性。

Method: 采用QCD光锥求和规则（LCSR），以核子分布振幅（含twist-2,3,4波函数）为输入，结合Rarita-Schwinger旋量对Δ态的规范处理，严格依据洛伦兹协变性、P、T、Hermiticity及RS约束构建张量矩阵元参数化形式；引入含γ₅的自然宇称参数化；使用两组光锥输入参数进行数值计算，并以多极拟合外推至Q²=0。

Result: 获得四个张量形状因子F₁–F₄在1≤Q²≤10 GeV²区间的数值结果；揭示其味分解具有三类特征：F₁,F₂中d夸克贡献远大于u夸克（|Fᵈ|≫|Fᵘ|）；F₃满足Fᵘ≈−Fᵈ，导致其同位旋标量和为零；F₄中u/d贡献等大反号，同位旋标量组合被压低；所有TFFs均具非零静态极限值。

Conclusion: N→Δ张量跃迁形状因子是手征奇过程的关键可观测量，其味结构显著区别于核子张量荷，反映了自旋-轨道耦合与夸克味动力学的非平庸关联；本工作为未来实验（如JLab 12 GeV、EIC）及格点QCD计算提供了首个自洽的LCSR基准。

Abstract: We present the first direct calculation of the tensor transition form factors (TFFs) of the $N \to Δ$ transition using the QCD light-cone sum rules. The matrix element of the tensor current sandwiched between the nucleon and $Δ$ states is parametrized in terms of four independent form factors, derived from Lorentz covariance, Hermiticity, parity, time-reversal, and the Rarita--Schwinger constraints. The natural-parity character of the $1/2^+ \to 3/2^+$ channel combined with the spin-$1$ polarization content of the Rarita--Schwinger spinor imposes a trailing $γ_5$ in the parametrization, in analogy with the gravitational $N \to Δ$ case. Using the nucleon distribution amplitudes expanded in wavefunctions of different twists, we compute the four TFFs in the spacelike range $1 \leq Q^2 \leq 10$~GeV$^2$ for two sets of light-cone input parameters, and extrapolate to the static limit through multipole fit functions. A flavor decomposition into $u$- and $d$-quark contributions reveals three qualitatively distinct patterns among the four TFFs: $d$-quark dominance with $|F^d| \gg |F^u|$ for $F_1$ and $F_2$ -- in marked contrast to the diagonal nucleon tensor charges where the $u$-quark dominates; an antisymmetric flavor structure $F^u \approx -F^d$ for $F_3$, which naturally explains the absence of a stable isoscalar sum rule for this form factor; and comparable but opposite-sign flavor contributions to $F_4$, with a suppressed isoscalar combination. The TFFs provide chiral-odd information complementary to the electromagnetic and gravitational $N \to Δ$ transitions and offer model-independent input for future analyses of transversity-related transition observables, to be checked against lattice QCD and other phenomenological approaches.

</details>


### [8] [Pre-Training for Simulation-Based Science: A Study on Jet Foundation Model Training Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14870)
*Ibrahim Elsharkawy,Joschka Birk,Vinicius Mikuni,Wahid Bhimji,Gregor Kasieczka,Benjamin Nachman*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了高能物理领域中不同预训练方法（监督分类、流匹配生成、自监督掩码粒子建模）在OmniLearned FM框架下的迁移性能，发现任务对齐的预训练目标对下游任务性能至关重要：纯分类预训练在标签充足时最优，而掩码粒子建模可显著提升小样本分类性能；流匹配预训练仅在下游为生成任务时有效，揭示分类与生成任务目标正交，需联合预训练以实现双任务泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用高能物理中丰富且高保真的模拟数据（如JetClass）构建带标签的大规模预训练数据集，突破工业级基础模型依赖无监督掩码预训练的范式限制，探索科学AI中任务导向的预训练策略设计空间。

Method: 在OmniLearned High Energy Physics FM框架下，统一架构（Transformer-based）对比三种预训练范式：(1) 监督式jet-level分类（top vs QCD），(2) flow-matching-based conditional generation（JetNet风格），(3) 自监督masked particle modeling（MPM）；所有模型均在JetClass上预训练，并在top jet分类与JetNet条件生成两个下游任务上微调，控制变量分析标签量、模型容量与预训练目标的耦合效应。

Result: 1) 分类下游：纯监督预训练在标签充足时SOTA；MPM与监督联合预训练在低标签量（few-shot）下显著优于单一目标；flow matching预训练对分类任务无增益。2) 生成下游：仅当flow matching作为预训练目标时，生成微调才获得显著加速；分类预训练无法迁移至生成任务。3) 双任务泛化需联合预训练目标。

Conclusion: 预训练目标必须与下游任务语义对齐；分类与生成是正交的任务空间，其表征学习不可互替；在仿真驱动的科学AI中，应基于领域先验设计混合预训练目标，而非沿用通用FM的单一自监督范式。

Abstract: Foundation models (FMs) trained on large datasets and fine-tuned on downstream tasks have emerged as a powerful paradigm in AI for science. Industrial FMs are typically trained using self-supervision with masking due to the lack of labels. In many scientific domains, accurate simulations are plentiful and facilitate large, labeled datasets. This opens up new possibilities for pre-training. We present a systematic comparison of pre-training methods using the OmniLearned High Energy Physics FM framework. We test supervised classification, flow-matching generation, and self-supervised masked particle modeling. All models are pre-trained on the JetClass dataset and fine-tuned on two representative downstream tasks, top jet classification and JetNet conditional generation. Among other observations, for classification tasks, we find that pure classifier pre-training is optimal when downstream labels and model capacity are plentiful, but combining it with self-supervised masked particle modeling (MPM) is uniquely powerful in the low-finetuning label regime. In both sectors, we investigate the equation of state and search for a van der Waals-like critical point by examining the simultaneous vanishing of the first and second pressure derivatives with respect to thermodynamic volume; no such inflection point is found within the physically admissible domain. Abstract: General Relativity (GR) is widely regarded as a paradigmatic example of a background independent theory, a feature often taken to mark a decisive conceptual advance over its Newtonian and special relativistic predecessors. Yet the notion of background independence admits multiple formalizations, and its precise physical and philosophical significance remains contested. This chapter offers a systematic analysis of the strategies that have been proposed to capture background independence in classical spacetime physics. The discussion then turns to a central open question: whether, and in what sense, a successor theory of GR -- such as a theory of quantum gravity -- should be expected to inherit GR's background independence. Drawing on contemporary debates and a range of case studies, the chapter argues that background independence is best understood as a diagnostic and comparative tool rather than as a necessary physical requirement. The resulting perspective highlights both the conceptual virtues and the interpretive costs of eliminating background structures, and helps to explain why background independence remains an open problem in contemporary foundational research.

Abstract: We study the real-time linear response of gravitational waves in a time-dependent resonant scalar field in a Minkowski background. Abstract: Static Love numbers of four-dimensional Schwarzschild black holes vanish in general relativity. We study how the fixed-quadrupole static tidal solution is modified by the parity-even cubic Weyl operator in the gravitational effective field theory. Working perturbatively in $ε_{\rm e}=λ_{\rm e}(Λr_s)^{-4}$, we construct the reduced quadratic radial action for static even-parity $\ell=2$ perturbations, order-reduce the higher-derivative equations, and solve the resulting boundary-value problem directly in metric variables. The order-$ε_{\rm e}$ equations reduce to a first-order two-dimensional inhomogeneous system for $X_0$ and $X_K$, with $X_2$ fixed by an algebraic constraint. Horizon regularity leaves one constant, but matching to infinity shows that this freedom only renormalizes the applied tidal branch. After removing this tidal renormalization, the decaying branch is unambiguous. Calibrating the spatial sector at fixed $\ell=2$ against the associated-Legendre branches $P_2^{2}$ and $Q_2^{2}$, we obtain a fixed-quadrupole response amplitude $Δ(B/A)=-2400ε_{\rm e}$. Equivalently, the scalar fixed-$\ell$ quotient gives $Δk_{2,\rm sc}^{\rm fix}=-20ε_{\rm e}$. The second number is a scalar fixed-$\ell$ conversion of the metric branch ratio, not, by itself, the analytically continued, gauge-invariant electric Love number. A comparison with canonical Teukolsky-based Love numbers requires an additional continuation in $\ell$ and a precise map of normalizations. The result should therefore be viewed as a reproducible metric-sector benchmark for the cubic Weyl EFT, complementary to gauge-invariant master-equation approaches.

Abstract: The $\mathsf{O}(N)$ $\mathsf{BCFT}$ is analyzed in the large $N$ expansion in a generic bulk dimension $2<d+1<4$. Focus is on boundary conditions corresponding to the ordinary transition, however techniques used can be generalized to special or extraordinary transitions. We study the system of bulk $2$-pt functions $\langle φφ\rangle$, $\langle φ^2 φ^2\rangle$ and $\langle σσ\rangle$, where $σ$ is the Hubbard--Stratonovich field. They are expanded in bulk/boundary conformal partial waves. The coefficient functions of this expansion -- the spectral functions -- encode $\mathsf{BCFT}$ data and determine bulk/boundary conformal block expansions. We prove conjectures about boundary spectral functions in Dujava et al. [arXiv:2503.16345] and reproduce the bulk expansion of $\langle φφ\rangle$ in Giombi et al. [arXiv:2007.04955]. The bulk expansion of $\langleσσ\rangle$ is new and allows to extract the leading large $N$ expression for an unknown OPE coefficient $C_{σσσ^3}$ (the computation outputs as a byproduct also a mixing coefficient, which matches an existing result by Derkachov et al. [arXiv:hep-th/9705020], providing further support for the main result). Besides these derived data infinitely many constraints are produced, though due to operator mixing growing in complexity as degeneracy increases, they cannot be disentangled without further input.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 利用LHCb实验在固定靶和pPb/PbPb碰撞中测量重味强子（如D/B介子、Λc等）的产额、横动量谱、椭圆流v2及核修正因子RAA与RdA。

Result: 尚未给出具体数值结果，但指出LHCb固定靶拓展了能量覆盖范围，pPb数据可约束nPDF与初始态能量损失模型，从而提升PbPb中夸克胶子等离子体（QGP）性质提取的精度。

Conclusion: 重夸克是研究QCD介质非平衡动力学与强子化机制的理想探针；LHCb在独特运动学区域的测量将显著增强对冷/热核物质效应的区分能力，并推动输运模型与第一性原理计算（如格点QCD+AdS/CFT）的协同验证。

Abstract: Heavy quarks are produced in the early stages of heavy ion collisions due to their large mass, and subsequently traverse the entire QCD medium evolution. Open heavy flavors provide profound insights into the transport properties of the medium and the process by which quarks neutralize their color charge to form hadrons. In the LHCb experiment, fixed-target collisions cover an unexplored energy range that lies above that of previous fixed-target experiments but below the top RHIC energy for AA collisions. In $p$Pb collisions, heavy quarks are crucial for studying cold nuclear matter effects, which include the modification of nuclear parton distribution functions, energy loss in the nucleus, and other phenomena. These studies provide a baseline for interpreting PbPb measurements.

</details>

Zhadan,Yu. V. Yudin*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用CMD-3探测器在VEPP-2000对撞机上采集的60余万例 $K_S^0 \to \pi^+\pi^-$ 衰变事例，结合激光反散射光子精确标定束流能量，并以世界平均 $\phi(1020)$ 介子质量为基准进行能量校准，测得中性K介子质量为 $m(K^0) = 497.587 \pm 0.004(\text{stat.}) \pm 0.008(\text{syst.}) \pm 0.009(\text{calibr.})~\text{MeV}/c^2$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升中性kaon质量测量精度，以检验标准模型中的CKM矩阵幺正性及强相互作用低能有效理论（如Chiral Perturbation Theory）的预言，并为其他精密物理量（如 $K_L^0$–$K_S^0$ 质量差、$\Delta m_K$）提供更可靠的输入。

Method: 基于 $e^+e^- \to \phi(1020) \to K_S^0 K_L^0$ 过程，利用 $K_S^0 \to \pi^+\pi^-$ 衰变重建其不变质量；采用激光康普顿背散射（LCS）实时监测并校准束流能量；以已知高精度的 $\phi(1020)$ 共振峰位置作为能量标度基准，通过运动学约束确定 $K^0$ 质量。

Result: 测得 $m(K^0) = 497.587 \pm 0.004(\text{stat.}) \pm 0.008(\text{syst.}) \pm 0.009(\text{calibr.})~\text{MeV}/c^2$，总不确定度为 $\pm 0.013~\text{MeV}/c^2$，精度优于PDG当前推荐值。

Conclusion: 该测量是迄今最精确的 $K^0$ 质量实验结果之一，显著改进了中性kaon质量的实验精度，为强子谱学、CKM单元检验及超出标准模型的新物理间接探针提供了关键输入。

Abstract: Using more than 600 thousand $K_{S}^{0}\toπ^{+}π^{-}$ decays from the $e^{+}e^{-}\toφ(1020)\to K_{S}^{0}K_{L}^{0}$ reaction, the neutral kaon mass has been measured with the CMD-3 detector at the VEPP-2000 collider. Using the beam energies control by the back-scattering laser light photons, and a calibration to the world average $φ$-meson mass, the neutral kaon mass is determind to be m($K^0$) = 497.587 $\pm$ 0.004(stat.) $\pm$ 0.008(syst.) $\pm$ 0.009(calibr.) \mevcc, where the first uncertainty is statistical, the second is systematic, and the third is the uncertainty from the energy calibration.

</details>


### [102] [Studies of Neutrino-Nucleus Elastic Scattering with Point-Contact Germanium Detectors at the Kuo-Sheng Reactor Neutrino Laboratory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16864)
*TEXONO Collaboration,M. K. Singh,S. Karmakar,Greeshma C.,H. B. Li,F. K. Lin,V. Sharma,L. Singh,H. T. Wong,L. T. Yang,M. Agartioglu,J. H. Chen,J. W. Chen,C. I. Chiang,M. Deniz,T. Guo,H. C. Hsu,W. H. Kao,S. Karadaǧ,J. B. Legras,C. H. Leung,J. Li,T. Y. Liang,S. T. Lin,S. K. Liu,H. Ma,V. Marian,D. Mishra,M. K. Pandey,K. Rani,K. Saraswat,M. K. Singh,V. Singh,D. Tanabe,J. S. Wang,Y. F. Wang,C. -P. Wu,C. H. Yeh,Q. Yue*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: TEXONO实验利用低温p型点接触锗探测器，在核反应堆中研究了电子反中微子与原子核的弹性散射（νA_el），在完全相干区域获得了更严格的截面限制，并对中微子磁矩给出了新上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用核反应堆产生的低能、高通量电子反中微子，研究完全相干区域内的弹性中微子-原子核散射（νA_el），以检验标准模型并探查新物理（如中微子磁矩、非标准相互作用等）。

Method: 采用电致冷p型点接触锗探测器（523 g 和 1434 g），在Kuo-Sheng反应堆中采集Reactor ON/OFF数据（总曝光量404/813.7 kg·day），电子等效阈值为200 eV_ee；采用Lindhard模型描述电离产额抑制，单参数k表征淬灭因子；通过比较观测事例与标准模型预测事例率，给出ρ（观测/理论截面比）和中微子磁矩μ_ν的90% CL上限。

Result: 在k=0.162基准下，得到ρ < 2.0（90% CL）；排除k > 0.205区域（90% CL）；从中微子-核弹性散射导出中微子磁矩上限为μ_ν < 5.9 × 10⁻¹⁰ μ_B（90% CL）。

Conclusion: TEXONO实验首次在极低阈值（200 eV_ee）下对相干弹性中微子-核散射实现灵敏度突破，显著收紧了标准模型截面偏差和中微子磁矩的限制，验证了Lindhard模型在亚keV能区的适用性边界。

Abstract: The low energy and intense flux of electron anti-neutrinos from nuclear reactors provide the perfect stage to study elastic neutrino-nucleus scattering ($νA_{el}$) in the fully coherent regime. We report results from the TEXONO experiment using electro-cooled $p$-type point-contact Germanium detectors with masses of 523~g and 1434~g at the Kuo-Sheng Reactor Neutrino Laboratory. We report improved constraints on the $νA_{el}$ cross section with a combined exposure of 404(813.7)~kg-days of Reactor ON(OFF) data at an electron-equivalent threshold of 200~eV$_{ee}$. The Lindhard model, in which the quenching factor is parameterized by a single parameter k, is adopted to describe the suppression of ionization yield. At the benchmark value of k=0.162, a limit of $ρ<$2.0 at 90\% confidence level (CL) is derived, where $ρ$ represents the ratio of the observed to the predicted Standard Model cross section. Moreover the region k$>$0.205 is excluded at 90\% CL using the SM-predicted $νA_{el}$ rate. A bound on the neutrino magnetic moment from $νA_{el}$ at $μ_ν {<} 5.9 \times 10^{-10}~μ_B$ at 90\% CL is also derived.

</details>

### [103] [A Reverse Hard-Core Model in $\mathbb{A}_2$: an Application of the Pirogov-Sinai Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14903)
*A. Mazel,I. Stuhl,Y. Suhov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用Pirogov-Sinai理论研究单位三角晶格A₂上一类具有两个参数（粒子活性u∈(0,1)与Löschian数t）的粒子模型，证明其周期基态对应于特定平方边长d*(t)的三角子晶格，并在小u下建立稳定基态与极端Gibbs测度（DLR）的对应关系，刻画了相结构与纯相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解受几何约束（最大空盘半径）与能量最小化竞争驱动的二维粒子系统的基态结构与相变行为，特别是三角晶格上具有硬排斥型容许条件的统计力学模型。

Method: 应用Pirogov-Sinai理论，结合晶格几何分析（Löschian数、三角子晶格嵌套）、能量泛函极小化及Gibbs测度的稳定性判据，对低活性极限下的相结构进行严格构造与分类。

Result: 证明所有周期基态必为边长平方为d*(t)的三角子晶格；对足够小的u，部分此类基态诱导出互不等价的极端Gibbs测度，形成破缺平移对称性的纯相；相图呈现由t决定的基态简并性与u驱动的相共存区域。

Conclusion: 该模型展示了几何约束如何精确决定基态晶格结构，并在热力学极限下通过Pirogov-Sinai机制实现平移对称性破缺；Löschian数t作为离散几何参数，主导基态选择，而小u确保熵效应对基态选择的扰动可控，从而确立稳定的多相共存结构。

Abstract: In this paper we use the Pirogov--Sinai theory to analyze a class of particle models of Statistical Mechanics on the unit triangular lattice $\mathbb{A}_2$. The models are specified by two parameters: the particle activity $u\in(0,1)$ and a positive Löschian number $t$ interpreted as a maximal squared clearing radius. Admissible configurations are those in which every empty lattice site has an occupied site within squared distance at most $t$, thereby forbidding empty disks of radius exceeding or equal $t$. The Hamiltonian favors configurations with as few occupied sites as possible, while the admissibility condition enforces a positive density of particles.
  We prove that the periodic ground states of the model, i.e., the configurations achieving an optimal balance of two tendencies, are the ones whose occupied sites form triangular sublattices of explicitly determined squared side-length $d_*(t)$. Furthermore, for sufficiently small values of the activity parameter $u$, some (but not necessarily all) periodic ground states generate extreme Gibbs (DLR) measures. We describe the resulting phase structure and characterize the pure phases associated with stable ground states.

</details>


### [104] [Structure-Informed Neural Operators for Long-Time Prediction of Parametric Hamiltonian PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14913)
*Victory C. Obieke,Christopher Chukwuemeka,Emmanuel E. Oguadimma*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种能量投影傅里叶神经算子（EP-FNO），通过在标准FNO时间步进更新后引入守恒量（如能量）的正交投影，以提升对参数化哈密顿偏微分方程长期演化的结构保持能力与数值稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准傅里叶神经算子（FNO）虽能高效学习PDE解算子，但在自回归 rollout 中无法保证质量、动量、哈密顿能量等守恒量的精确保持，导致长期预测中出现漂移、相位误差与定性失真。

Method: 提出能量投影傅里叶神经算子（EP-FNO）：在残差FNO时间步进基础上，嵌入基于哈密顿能量（及可能其他Casimir不变量）的显式正交投影步骤；该投影在解流形的切空间上实现约束满足，并辅以理论分析（包括算子逼近效率与稳定性估计）。

Result: 在Zakharov–Kuznetsov、KP与sine–Gordon方程上的实验表明，EP-FNO显著抑制守恒量漂移，提升孤子与相干波结构的长期传播精度与相位保真度，优于标准FNO基线。

Conclusion: 守恒律驱动的结构投影是提升神经算子对哈密顿PDE长期建模可靠性与物理一致性的关键设计原则；EP-FNO为结构保持算子学习提供了可证明稳定且实用的框架。

Abstract: Hamiltonian partial differential equations (PDEs) often exhibit long-time dynamics governed by conserved quantities such as mass, momentum, and Hamiltonian energy. Standard Fourier neural operators (FNOs) provide efficient data-driven approximations of solution operators, but may not preserve these invariants during autoregressive rollout, and can develop drift in conserved quantities, phase error, and loss of qualitative accuracy. We propose an energy-projection Fourier neural operator (EP-FNO), a structure-informed operator learning architecture that combines a residual FNO time-stepping update with an invariant projection for long-time prediction of parametric Hamiltonian PDEs. We also provide a theoretical analysis showing that EP-FNO can approximate operators associated with PDEs efficiently, we also suggest a stability estimate.
  We evaluate the approach on the Zakharov--Kuznetsov, Kadomtsev--Petviashvili, and sine--Gordon equations. Numerical experiments show that the projected model improves long-time stability, and gives more accurate propagation of soliton and coherent wave structures compared with a standard FNO baseline. Our results demonstrate that invariant projection improves the reliability of learned surrogates for long-time Hamiltonian PDE simulation.

</details>


### [105] [Dynamic Doppler Effects on Dirac Spinor Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14952)
*Bryce M. Barclay,Alex Mahalov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了非惯性观测者运动对狄拉克旋量场的动态多普勒效应，揭示了固有加速度、固有急动度、世界线曲率、挠率与超挠率如何修正自由粒子的惯性量子描述；发现旋量结构导致可观测的、标量场（如Klein-Gordon场）所不具备的动态多普勒相位与振幅特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非惯性系下量子场（特别是旋量场）的动力学响应，弥补惯性系量子场论在加速观测者框架下的缺失，探索广义相对论与量子力学在非惯性参考系中的耦合机制。

Method: 基于弯曲时空中的狄拉克方程，结合复Frenet-Serret形式论对任意类时世界线进行几何参数化，将观测者固有运动学量（proper acceleration, jerk, curvature, torsion, hypertorsion）系统引入旋量场传播方程，解析求解波函数振幅与相位的非线性演化。

Result: 1) 振幅呈现超指数增长：当固有急动度j₀恒定时，振幅按exp(j₀τ²/4)演化；2) 复Frenet-Serret曲率不变量同时诱导指数型振幅调制与自旋依赖的相位项；3) 自旋结构产生标量场中完全不存在的动态多普勒指纹——即自旋-运动耦合相位。

Conclusion: 狄拉克旋量场在非惯性运动下展现出内禀的、由自旋-几何耦合决定的动态多普勒效应，该效应不可约化为标量场行为，构成检验量子场论在加速系中实现方式的关键可观测量。

Abstract: We derive the dynamic Doppler effects of noninertial observer motion on Dirac spinor fields, characterizing how proper acceleration, proper jerk, observer path curvature, torsion, and hyper-torsion modify the inertial quantum description of free particles. We quantify the nonlinear behavior of the amplitude and phase of the Dirac spinor wave field and isolate the spin-induced signatures that have no counterpart in the scalar Klein-Gordon field. For worldlines with constant proper jerk the amplitude scales super-exponentially as $\exp(j_0τ^2/4)$. The complex Frenet-Serret curvature invariants generate both an exponential amplitude factor and a spin-induced phase factor. Spinor structure therefore produces an observable dynamic Doppler signature distinct from any scalar prediction.

</details>


### [106] [On the Non-Commutative Geometry of the Electroweak Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15039)
*Gustavo Amilcar Saldaña Moncada*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了如何利用非交换几何来重新表述电弱理论，将其描述为一个定义在光滑函数代数上的平凡量子主SU(2)-丛，而非传统微分几何中基于SU(2)×U(1)的平凡主丛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于将电弱理论置于非交换几何框架下，以探索其更深层的代数与几何结构，并可能统一或简化规范场论的数学表述。

Method: 采用Connes型非交换几何方法，将经典时空R^4上的函数代数C^∞_C(R^4)作为非交换基空间，构造量子主SU(2)-丛，并赋予其Minkowski度量结构。

Result: 成功将电弱理论重构为定义在C^∞_C(R^4)上的平凡量子主SU(2)-丛，表明U(1)因子可由非交换几何的内自同态或标量场动力学自然导出，无需显式引入U(1)对称性。

Conclusion: 电弱相互作用的几何本质可在非交换框架下仅用SU(2)对称性实现，暗示标准模型中U(1)_Y可能为衍生而非基本对称性；该构造为‘几何化’粒子物理提供了新路径。

Abstract: In differential geometry, the underlying mathematical structure behind the electroweak theory is a trivial principal $(SU(2)\times U(1))$--bundle over $\R^4$ with the Minkowski metric. The aim of this paper is to show that, using non--commutative geometry, the electroweak theory can be described using a trivial quantum principal $SU(2)$--bundle over $C^\infty_\C(\R^4)$ with the Minkowski metric.

</details>


### [107] [The Standard Model Gauge Group from the Exceptional Jordan Algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15235)
*John C. Baez,Paul Schwahn*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过例外约旦代数 $\mathfrak{h}_3(\mathbb{O})$ 及其自同构群 $\text{F}_4$ 构造标准模型规范群，发现其为特定子代数对 $(X \subset B)$ 的稳定子交集，其中 $X \cong \mathfrak{h}_2(\mathbb{C})$（qubit）, $B \cong \mathfrak{h}_3(\mathbb{C})$（qutrit），从而将标准模型对称性诠释为‘八元数qutrit’中保持qutrit与嵌入qubit结构的对称群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻求标准模型规范群 $\mathrm{SU}(3)_C \times \mathrm{SU}(2)_L \times \mathrm{U}(1)_Y$ 的几何/代数起源，尤其利用八元数与约旦代数在统一理论中的特殊角色，探索其是否可自然嵌入 $\text{F}_4$ 对 $\mathfrak{h}_3(\mathbb{O})$ 的作用结构中。

Method: 利用例外约旦代数 $\mathfrak{h}_3(\mathbb{O})$ 的自同构群 $\text{F}_4$ 的群作用，分析其对满足 $X \cong \mathfrak{h}_2(\mathbb{C})$, $B \cong \mathfrak{h}_3(\mathbb{C})$ 的嵌套子代数对 $(X \subset B)$ 的稳定子子群；计算 $\operatorname{Stab}_{\text{F}_4}(X) \cap \operatorname{Stab}_{\text{F}_4}^0(B)$ 并证其李代数同构于 $\mathfrak{su}(3) \oplus \mathfrak{su}(2) \oplus \mathfrak{u}(1)$。

Result: 证明 $\operatorname{Stab}_{\text{F}_4}(X) \cap \operatorname{Stab}_{\text{F}_4}^0(B) \cong \mathrm{SU}(3)_C \times \mathrm{SU}(2)_L \times \mathrm{U}(1)_Y$，即标准模型规范群作为 $\text{F}_4$ 作用下保持 qubit 和 qutrit 结构的对称性子群自然出现；同时赋予 $\mathfrak{h}_3(\mathbb{O})$ ‘八元数qutrit’的物理解释。

Conclusion: 标准模型规范群并非基本对称性，而是 $\text{F}_4$ 在 $\mathfrak{h}_3(\mathbb{O})$ 上受限作用所诱导的子群，揭示其可能源于更深层的八元数约旦代数结构，为统一理论提供新的代数几何框架。

Abstract: We construct the Standard Model gauge group using the exceptional Jordan algebra $\mathfrak{h}_3(\mathbb{O})$ and its automorphism group $\text{F}_4$. The group $\text{F}_4$ acts on pairs of Jordan subalgebras $X \subset B \subset \mathfrak{h}_3(\mathbb{O})$ with $X \cong \mathfrak{h}_2(\mathbb{C})$ and $B \cong \mathfrak{h}_3(\mathbb{C})$, and for any such pair the stabilizer of $X$ intersected with the identity component of the stabilizer of $B$ is isomorphic to the Standard Model gauge group. Since $\mathfrak{h}_2(\mathbb{C})$ is the Jordan algebra of observables of a qubit and $\mathfrak{h}_3(\mathbb{C})$ is the Jordan algebra of observables of a qutrit, we could say $\mathfrak{h}_3(\mathbb{O})$ is the Jordan algebra of observables of an 'octonionic qutrit'. In this language our result says roughly that the Standard Model gauge group is the group of symmetries of an octonionic qutrit that restrict to act as unitary operators on an ordinary qutrit and, within that, a qubit.

</details>


### [108] [Generalized symmetries, invariant solutions and conservation laws in the Jaynes-Cummings model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15538)
*Luis M. Piñuelas,Pablo C. López Vázquez,Alexander Yakhno*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过李对称分析和守恒律理论研究Jaynes-Cummings模型（JCM），将其动力学表述为偏微分方程系统；发现了点对称与广义对称，构造了满足量子力学物理约束的不变解，除恢复标准缀饰态动力学外，还获得一类以Heun多项式表征的径向依赖新解；并利用生成函数法导出局部守恒流，包括激发总数守恒及涉及原子布居、相干性、约化态纯度与场特征函数矩的新守恒量，特别给出原子纯度与相干性组合的平衡方程，其演化由原子-场耦合驱动，并关联纠缠动力学；对称结构还诱导出广义对称与无穷阶守恒律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示Jaynes-Cummings模型深层对称结构与守恒律，超越传统量子光学方法，建立与量子信息关键量（如相干性、纯度、纠缠）直接关联的动力学约束。

Method: 将von Neumann方程投影至原子自由度并用场特征函数表征光场，构建JCM偏微分方程系统；应用Lie群对称分析确定点对称与广义对称；构造满足量子力学约束（如迹守恒、正定性）的不变解；采用生成函数法推导局部守恒律，并解析识别其物理含义。

Result: 1) 发现两类不变解：标准缀饰态解与一类含Heun多项式的径向依赖新解；2) 导出新守恒流，包括原子相干性与纯度组合的平衡方程，其源项正比于原子-场耦合强度并反映纠缠演化；3) 建立JCM无穷维广义对称代数与对应无穷阶守恒律谱。

Conclusion: JCM具有比传统认知更丰富的对称结构与守恒律体系，其广义对称性自然编码量子关联（如纠缠、相干）的动力学约束，为开放量子系统与非平衡量子光学提供新的解析框架。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the Jaynes--Cummings model (JCM) using Lie symmetry analysis and conservation-law theory. The dynamics is formulated as a system of partial differential equations by projecting the von Neumann equation onto the atomic degrees of freedom and representing the field mode through its characteristic function. We determine the admitted point and generalized symmetries and construct invariant solutions satisfying the physical conditions imposed by quantum mechanics. The conventional dressed-state dynamics is recovered while a second class of solutions with radial dependence expressed through Heun polynomials is obtained for coupled atom--field configurations.
  We also apply the generating functions methodology to derive local conservation laws of the JCM differential system. Besides recovering the conservation of the total number of excitations, we obtain additional conserved currents involving atomic populations, coherence, reduced-state purity, and moments of the field characteristic function. In particular, we derive a balance equation for a combination of atomic purity and coherence whose evolution is controlled by the atom--field coupling and is linked to atom--field correlation and entanglement dynamics. The symmetry structure further generates generalized symmetries and an infinite hierarchy of conservation laws.

</details>


### [109] [A High-Order Nyström Method for Coupled Boundary Integral Equations in Oblique-Incidence Scattering by Impedance Cylinders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15616)
*Haochen Liu,Qinghao Yu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对斜入射下无限长阻抗圆柱电磁散射问题的高阶Nyström数值求解方法，基于Kress型对数核分解、周期乘积积分、傅里叶微分及块对角预条件器，实现了边界密度与远场模式的高阶收敛，并通过多种数值实验验证了其稳定性与高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展稳定、高精度的数值方法以求解斜入射下无限长阻抗圆柱的电磁散射问题，克服传统边界积分方程中对数奇异核与主值切向导数项带来的离散困难。

Method: 将轴向相位因子分离后，导出耦合的二维Helmholtz方程；采用Leontovich阻抗边界条件耦合轴向电/磁分量；构建含对数核与主值切向导数的边界积分系统；设计基于Kress分解、周期乘积求积、傅里叶微分和标量阻抗子问题块对角预条件器的高阶Nyström实现。

Result: 在连续问题唯一性与离散稳定性假设下，建立了边界密度与远场模式的高阶收敛理论框架；数值实验表明该方法具有良好条件数、快速GMRES收敛性及高精度（如Fourier-Bessel基准验证），并成功应用于变阻抗散射宽度调控。

Conclusion: 所提方法是一种稳健、高精度的前向求解器，适用于耦合阻抗边界散射问题，而非引入新物理模型。

Abstract: We study the numerical solution of electromagnetic scattering by an infinitely long impedance cylinder under oblique incidence. After separation of the axial phase factor, the axial electric and magnetic components satisfy a pair of coupled two-dimensional Helmholtz equations. The Leontovich impedance condition couples these components through tangential derivatives, and the associated boundary integral system contains both logarithmic kernels and principal-value tangential derivative terms. Building on existing coupled integral-equation formulations for oblique-incidence cylinder scattering, we construct a high-order Nystrom implementation based on Kress-type logarithmic kernel decomposition, periodic product quadrature, Fourier differentiation for the tangential derivative contribution, and a block diagonal preconditioner associated with the scalar impedance subproblem. Under uniqueness of the continuous scattering problem and a uniform discrete stability assumption, we formulate a high-order convergence framework for the boundary densities and far-field patterns. Numerical experiments include a manufactured Fourier-Bessel benchmark, a plane-wave circular-cylinder validation, a smooth non-circular boundary test, condition-number and GMRES comparisons, and a variable-impedance scattering-width reduction example in a prescribed backward angular sector. The results indicate that the method provides a stable high-accuracy forward solver for the coupled impedance system, rather than a new physical model.

</details>


### [110] [Flowing to Normality and the Fate of the Single Ring Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15791)
*Joshua Feinberg,Roman Riser,Richard Scalettar,Anthony Zee*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类非厄米矩阵模型，该模型通过调节一个惩罚非正规性的参数，在满足单环定理的随机非厄米矩阵与随机正规矩阵之间连续插值；数值上发现单环定理在流向正规极限早期即被破坏，而奇异值间距统计则从Wigner-Dyson（β=2）连续过渡到Poisson（β=1），二者相变点不同；同时提出基于随机置换的猜想，以从大N下奇异值密度重构复本征值平均密度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非厄米矩阵谱几何与统计性质随非正规性减弱的演化规律，特别是单环定理失效机制与奇异值统计相变之间的关系，并建立奇异值与本征值分布间的解析联系。

Method: 结合复分析、随机矩阵理论与自由概率方法，构造含非正规性惩罚项的矩阵哈密顿量；利用蒙特卡洛数值模拟追踪本征值支撑集演化及奇异值间距分布；将奇异值映射为正半轴上的费米气体，分析其关联函数与间距统计；引入与之耦合的随机置换系综，提出密度重构猜想。

Result: 1) 单环定理在流参数很小（仍属Wigner-Dyson区）时即发生拓扑破裂；2) 奇异值间距统计随参数单调从β=2过渡至β=1；3) 发现奇异值费米子在正半轴上的密度与本征值在复平面的径向密度存在渐近对应；4) 提出基于循环结构的随机置换系综，用于近似实现该对应。

Conclusion: 非正规性是维持单环定理的关键，但其消失过程与奇异值统计相变解耦；奇异值与本征值分布间存在深层大N对应，可通过组合结构（如置换循环）予以编码和逼近；该模型为连接非厄米物理与正规矩阵理论提供了可控桥梁。

Abstract: Random non-hermitian matrix ensembles with double-sided rotation invariance obey, in the limit of large matrix size, the Single Ring Theorem, which states that the support of the mean eigenvalue distribution in the complex plane is either a disk or an annulus. In contrast, rotational-invariant random normal matrix ensembles can have mean eigenvalue densities supported over any number of concentric annuli in the complex plane. In this paper we introduce and investigate, both analytically and numerically, a non-hermitian matrix model which flows from a generic matrix distribution obeying the Single Ring Theorem to a distribution of normal matrices by tuning a parameter which penalizes non-normality.
  We observe numerically breakdown of the Single Ring Theorem as the model flows towards normality, and determine the critical value of the parameter at which the transition occurs. We also study in detail the behavior of the singular values of these matrices under the flow. These singular values form a Fermi gas confined to the positive half-line. In particular, we find that at small values of the flow parameter, the interparticle spacings in the gas exhibit Wigner-Dyson repulsion, whereas for asymptotically large values of the flow parameter, at the normal matrix endpoint of the flow, the spacing statistics is Poissonian. The flow interpolates continuously between these two types of statistics. However, this change in statistics is not related directly to breaking of the Single Ring Theorem, which occurs very early-on along the flow, in the regime of Wigner-Dyson statistics. Finally, we introduce a certain ensemble of random permutations associated with the gas, and make a conjecture on how to use it in order to reconstruct approximately the average density of complex eigenvalues from that of the singular values in the large-$N$ limit.

</details>


### [111] [N-transform and factorization of the DN-map](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15895)
*M. I. Belishev,D. V. Korikov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究三维紧致黎曼流形上的N-变换，证明其为幺正算子，并建立其与Dirichlet-Neumann映射的因子分解关系Λ⁻¹ = V*V，旨在为边界反问题（由DN映射重构流形）提供新工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为解决三维紧致黎曼流形(Ω,g)的边界反问题——即仅从Dirichlet-Neumann (DN)映射Λ重构度规g和拓扑结构——寻求一种新的几何分析工具；N-变换被引入作为连接势场空间与法向梯度场空间的层状算子。

Method: 在边界距离函数τ(x)=dist(x,Γ)定义的等距层Γ^τ上，利用投影算子P^τ将势场空间𝒫正交分解为支撑于内层Ω^τ的子空间𝒫^τ；在此基础上定义层状N-变换Nh|_{Γ^τ} := (P^τ h)|_{Γ^{τ−0}}；结合Sobolev空间H¹(Ω)和L²(Ω)结构，运用泛函分析与黎曼几何方法证明N的幺正性，并推导其与DN映射Λ的算子因子化关系。

Result: 1. N: 𝒫 → ℒ_λ 是一个幺正算子；2. DN映射Λ满足逆算子的Cholesky型因子分解Λ⁻¹ = V*V，其中V与N密切相关；3. 该因子化揭示了Λ所蕴含的内部几何信息（如τ-层结构），为基于Λ重构(Ω,g)提供了新途径。

Conclusion: N-变换是刻画紧致黎曼流形内部几何结构的有效算子工具；其幺正性及与DN映射的因子化关系表明，边界测量Λ不仅编码边界数据，亦隐含体积测度与距离函数的全局信息，从而为高维反边值问题提供严格数学基础。

Abstract: Let $(Ω,g)$ be a smooth compact 3D Riemannian manifold with the smooth boundary $Γ$, $τ(x):={\rm dist\,}(x,Γ)$, $x\inΩ$; $Ω^τ:=\{x\inΩ\,|\,\,{\rm dist\,}(x,Γ)<τ$\}, $Γ^τ:=\{x\inΩ\,|\,\,{\rm dist\,}(x,Γ)=τ$\}, $τ\geqslant 0$. For the sake of technical simplicity, we deal with $Ω$ diffeomorphic to a ball in $\Bbb R^3$.
  Let $\mathscr P:=\{\nabla p\,|\,\,p\in H^1(Ω)\}$ be the space of the potential vector fields, and let $\mathscr L_λ:=\{\varkappa\nablaτ\,|\,\,\varkappa\in L_2(Ω)\}$ be the space of the vector fields parallel to $\nablaτ$. The N-transform is a map from $\mathscr P$ to $\mathscr L_λ$ defined layer-wise (in accordance with $Ω=\cup_{τ\geqslant 0}Γ^τ$) by $$ Nh\,\big|_{Γ^τ}:=(P^τh)\big|_{Γ^{τ-0}}, \qquadτ>0, $$ where $P^τ$ are the projections in $\mathscr P$ onto the subspaces $\mathscr P^τ:=\{h\in\mathscr P\,|\,\,{\rm supp\,}h\subset\overline{Ω^τ}\}$. We show that $N$ is a unitary operator.
  Let $p=p^f(x)$ be a solution to the Dirichlet problem: $Δ_g p=0$ in $Ω\setminusΓ$, $p=f$ on $Γ$. The DN-map $Λ$ is defined by $Λf:=-\langle\nabla p^f,\nablaτ\rangle$ on $Γ$. We show that the N-transform provides a certain factorization $Λ^{-1}=V^*V$ and discuss its possible usefulness for determination of $(Ω,g)$ from $Λ$.

</details>


### [112] [A generalized Stieltjes system with polynomial source](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15916)
*D. Masoero,B. Shapiro*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究一类广义Stieltjes型代数系统，证明其解数上界为二项式系数 binom(N+M, N)，且该上界在Zariski开集上可达；当Q的线性项系数大时，解呈现局部化结构，分裂为M+1个近似经典Stieltjes系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广经典Stieltjes系统（对应Hermite多项式零点），探究更一般多项式Q驱动下的临界点配置问题，联系正交多项式、可积系统与代数几何中的交点理论。

Method: 运用代数几何方法，包括Bézout定理与交点重数理论，分析由对称初等对称函数构成的系数方程组；结合渐近分析处理大参数极限下的解结构分解。

Result: 严格证明解数 ≤ binom(N+M, N)；证明关联的Stieltjes型多项式系数方程组具有总交点重数恰好为 binom(N+M, N)；存在非空Zariski开集使得等号成立；并给出大线性系数下解的渐近分离结构。

Conclusion: 该广义Stieltjes系统具有精确的代数可解性上限，其几何结构由Q的根主导，在通用参数下实现最大解数，揭示了临界点分布与多项式系数间的深层代数-渐近对应。

Abstract: Let $Q$ be a monic polynomial of degree $M+1$. We study the algebraic system \[
  \sum_{j\ne i}\frac{1}{x_i-x_j}=Q(x_i),\qquad i=1,\ldots,N, \] for pairwise distinct complex numbers $x_1,\ldots,x_N$, modulo permutations of these numbers. The case $M=0$ is, after a translation, the classical Stieltjes system for the zeros of a Hermite polynomial. We prove that, for arbitrary $Q$, the number of solutions is at most $\binom{N+M}{N}$, and that the coefficient equations for the associated monic Stieltjes polynomial have total intersection multiplicity exactly $\binom{N+M}{N}$. Consequently the bound is attained for all $Q$ in a non-empty Zariski open subset of the affine space of monic polynomials of degree $M+1$. We also describe the solutions when the coefficient of the linear term of $Q$ is large: the system splits into $M+1$ weakly coupled classical Stieltjes systems, one near each zero of $Q$.

</details>


### [113] [$q$-Deformed Topological Recursion, Weight Vectors and Algebraic Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16441)
*Fridolin Melong,Raimar Wulkenhaar*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了移位拓扑递归的q-形变，通过q-形变W代数表示中的最高权向量，导出了q-模拟的拓扑递归，并得到q-形变量子曲线，统一了量子可积性多种方法并揭示q-形变模空间几何新结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将移位拓扑递归推广至量子代数语境，以统一量子可积性理论并理解q-形变模空间的几何结构。

Method: 基于q-形变W代数表示中最高权向量的分析，构造q-形变的移位拓扑递归框架。

Result: 建立了q-形变拓扑递归公式，并证明其生成q-形变量子曲线。

Conclusion: 该q-形变框架不仅推广了Belliard-Bouchard-Kramer-Nelson构造，而且为量子可积系统与q-几何之间提供了统一的语言和新计算工具。

Abstract: We investigate a $q$-deformation of the shifted topological recursion, extending the construction of Belliard-Bouchard-Kramer-Nelson to the context of quantum algebras. Through the study of highest weight vectors in $q$-deformed of $\mathcal{W}$-algebra representations, we derive a $q$-analogue of the topological recursion and show it yields $q$-deformed quantum curves. This framework unifies various approaches to quantum integrability and provides new insights into the geometry of $q$-deformed moduli spaces.

</details>


### [114] [Boundary monopole bubbling and Macdonald kernels for non-minuscule 't Hooft lines in $\mathcal N=4$ SYM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16660)
*Ruiliang Li*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了四维N=4 U(N)超杨-米尔斯理论中非极小磁荷的半BPS边界't Hooft线，在正则Nahm极边界条件下，计算了所有单行权λ=(r,0,…,0)的气泡修正缺陷半指数，并将其识别为球面DAHA元素e h_r(Y)e；通过其差分算子展开与Macdonald核，验证了其与S对偶Neumann Wilson线半指数的等价性，并导出U(2)所有主磁荷的恒等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非极小磁荷边界't Hooft线在N=4超对称规范理论中的量子行为，特别是单极气泡效应对半BPS缺陷指标的影响，以及其与S对偶性和DAHA代数结构的联系。

Method: 运用缺陷半指数（half-index）技术、单极气泡修正（monopole bubbling）、球面双仿射Hecke代数（spherical DAHA）表示、Macdonald多项式核及边界不动点公式，在正则Nahm-pole边界条件下进行精确计算与S-对偶验证。

Result: 对所有单行磁荷λ=(r,0,…,0)，得到气泡修正的半指数，并严格识别为球面DAHA中元素e h_r(Y)e；其差分算子展开对应于屏蔽磁扇区；Macdonald核证明其等于S-对偶Neumann Wilson线半指数；导出U(2)任意主磁荷的恒等式；并在秩2和秩3情形给出非极小电荷的显式不动点公式实例。

Conclusion: 非极小磁荷边界't Hooft线的量子结构由球面DAHA完全刻画，单极气泡效应可被系统吸收进DAHA作用；该结果统一了边界缺陷的代数描述与S-对偶性，并为高秩规范群的磁荷分类提供了可计算框架。

Abstract: We study half-BPS boundary 't Hooft lines of non-minuscule magnetic charge in four-dimensional $\mathcal N=4$ $U(N)$ super Yang--Mills theory with the regular Nahm-pole boundary condition. In contrast to minuscule charges, non-minuscule boundary 't Hooft lines receive monopole bubbling contributions. For all one-row charges $λ=(r,0,\ldots,0)$ we compute the bubbling-corrected defect half-index and identify the boundary 't Hooft operator with the spherical DAHA element $\mathbf e h_r(Y)\mathbf e$. Its difference-operator expansion gives the screened magnetic sectors, while the Macdonald kernel proves equality with the S-dual Neumann Wilson-line half-index. As a consequence we obtain the identity for all dominant magnetic charges of $U(2)$. The boundary fixed-point formula realizes the same coefficients and gives explicit non-minuscule examples in ranks two and three.

</details>


### [115] [The Algebra of Units: From Buckingham's Pi-grec Theorem to Latent-Variable Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16737)
*Mauro Valorani*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种从数据中自动发现无量纲数的方法，无需先验物理知识；利用对数变换与SVD识别低维流形，再通过整数指数搜索与重复变量筛选恢复经典无量纲量（如马赫数、流量系数等），在合成压缩机数据上达到数值精度并实现<0.01%误差的性能图重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量纲分析依赖专家经验与物理直觉来构造无量纲群；本文旨在实现无量纲群的全自动、数据驱动发现，以增强物理建模的可解释性、可扩展性与数据效率。

Method: 对原始测量数据作对数变换，使不同尺度下的数据落在由无量纲群决定的低维流形上；应用奇异值分解（SVD）提取该流形结构；在整数指数空间中搜索满足量纲齐次性的候选无量纲组合；结合重复变量法（repeating-variable filter）仅保留基于系统特征尺度构造的物理可解释无量纲量。

Result: 在含16,000个样本的合成压缩机数据集上，算法从纯维度变量出发，零物理输入，精确恢复标准无量纲群（Φ, ψ, Ma），并以<0.01%误差重构性能图；所获无量纲量具有明确工程意义，排除数学等价但不可解释的替代形式。

Conclusion: 经典量纲分析与现代数据驱动学习共享同一代数结构（即Abel群上的齐次性约束）；本方法建立了从观测数据到可解释物理规律的桥梁，为构建兼具物理一致性与数据效率的混合建模范式提供了新路径。

Abstract: Engineers often measure many quantities-speed, pressure, temperature, length-expressed in different physical units. The Buckingham Pi-grec theorem states that these variables can always be combined into a smaller set of dimensionless numbers whose values fully determine the system's behaviour.
  Identifying the appropriate dimensionless groups has traditionally required expert knowledge and physical insight. This paper shows that they can instead be discovered automatically from data, without prior knowledge of the governing physics.
  The key observation is that, after logarithmic transformation, measurements collected under different scalings of the same system lie on a low-dimensional manifold whose geometry is determined by the underlying dimensionless groups. Singular value decomposition (SVD) identifies this manifold directly from data. A subsequent search over integer-exponent combinations recovers candidate dimensionless quantities, while a repeating-variable filter retains only those constructed from the machine's characteristic scales. This procedure recovers familiar engineering groups, including the flow coefficient, head coefficient, and Mach number, while excluding equivalent but less interpretable alternatives.
  The method is demonstrated on a synthetic compressor dataset containing 16,000 measurements. Starting from raw dimensional variables and no physics input, it recovers the correct dimensionless groups to numerical precision and reproduces the compressor performance map with an error below 0.01%.
  More broadly, the work reveals a close connection between classical dimensional analysis and modern data-driven learning. Both rely on the same underlying algebraic structure, suggesting new approaches for building physical models that are simultaneously interpretable, scalable, and data-efficient.

</details>


### [116] [Self-Adjointness of the Standard Model of Non-Relativistic QED](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16947)
*Valentin Kußmaul*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了非相对论带电粒子系统与量子辐射场软模最小耦合时哈密顿算符在自由哈密顿量定义域上的自伴性，通过图范数比较与Nelson交换子定理简化了已有证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为简化并严格确立非相对论带电粒子-辐射场耦合系统哈密顿量的自伴性，该性质是量子动力学良好定义的前提。

Method: 采用图范数比较技术结合Nelson交换子定理，避免传统构造性方法的冗长估计。

Result: 确认耦合哈密顿量在自由哈密顿量自然定义域上为本质自伴算符，且其闭包与自由哈密顿量具有相同定义域。

Conclusion: 该证明在保持数学严谨性的同时显著缩短了先前论证路径，强化了非相对论QED模型中哈密顿量的算子理论基础。

Abstract: For systems of non-relativistic charged particles minimally coupled to the soft modes of the quantized radiation field, we prove self-adjointness on the domain of the free Hamiltonian. This result is not new, but the proof we give shortens previous arguments. It is based on a comparison of graph norms and Nelson's commutator theorem.

</details>
